**[00:04:57.460] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-000065)  
We welcome our guests from the Commission on Audit, headed by our Chairperson, Gamaliel Cordoba.

**[00:05:07.420] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-000066)  
The Commission on Audit stands as a constitutional institution entrusted with a fundamental responsibility to safeguard the people's resources and ensure that public funds are collected, managed, and utilized in accordance with law.

**[00:05:28.560] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-000067)  
with accountability, transparency, economy, efficiency, and effectiveness.

**[00:05:39.380] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-000068)  
The proposed 2027 fiscal year budget of the Commission

**[00:05:45.900] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-000069)  
amounting to P16.046 billion

**[00:05:52.560] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-000070)  
is an investment in the country's system of public accountability,

**[00:06:01.700] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-000071)  
strengthen its audit operations,

**[00:06:05.100] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-000072)  
modernize its systems and processes,

**[00:06:11.120] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-000073)  
develop the capabilities of its personnel,

**[00:06:14.300] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-000074)  
and respond more effectively to the increasing complexity of government programs, transactions,

**[00:06:24.540] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-000075)  
and public expenditures.

**[00:06:27.720] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-000076)  
With the support of Congress,

**[00:06:31.840] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-000077)  
the Commission on Audit will continue to uphold its constitutional mandate with independence,

**[00:06:43.300] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-000078)  
professionalism, and integrity.

**[00:06:46.720] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-000079)  
We will strive to make

**[00:06:48.980] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-000080)  
public audit, not merely an exercise in detecting irregularities,

**[00:06:57.360] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-000081)  
but an instrument for improving government performance,

**[00:07:03.200] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-000082)  
preventing the wastage of public resources and strengthening public trust.

**[00:07:11.880] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-000095)  
The 16.046 billion proposed budget for fiscal year 2027 will help ensure that the Commission has the resources necessary to perform its mandate

**[00:07:29.640] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-000096)  
effectively and to remain a steadfast partner in promoting good governance and responsible stewardship of the nation's wealth.

**[00:07:42.240] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-000097)  
Maraming salamat po.

**[00:07:55.780] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-000098)  
Chairperson Gamaliel Cordoba, sir, you may now proceed with your presentation.

**[00:09:24.440] COA Assistant Commissioner Nilda B. Plaras** (asr-000107)  
For our financial performance for fiscal year 2025, COA received total allotment of 15,101,610,000 and this comprised of agency-specific budget.

**[00:09:43.980] COA Assistant Commissioner Nilda B. Plaras** (asr-000108)  
The special purpose fund, this is for the second trans-salary standardization.

**[00:09:49.940] COA Assistant Commissioner Nilda B. Plaras** (asr-000109)

**[00:09:59.400] COA Assistant Commissioner Nilda B. Plaras** (asr-000110)  
Then the retirement and life insurance premiums and the continuing appropriations.

**[00:10:05.020] COA Assistant Commissioner Nilda B. Plaras** (asr-000111)  
Out of this total allotment, we were able to obligate 14,702,701,000.

**[00:10:14.980] COA Assistant Commissioner Nilda B. Plaras** (asr-000112)  
or obligation rate of 97.36, resulting to a balance of unobligated allotment of 398,909,000.

**[00:10:30.660] COA Assistant Commissioner Nilda B. Plaras** (asr-000113)  
Out of the total obligations incurred, we dispersed 14,309,736,000, which is 97.33.

**[00:10:44.580] COA Assistant Commissioner Nilda B. Plaras** (asr-000114)  
Resulting to a balance of unpaid obligations of 392,965,000.

**[00:10:52.880] COA Assistant Commissioner Nilda B. Plaras** (asr-000115)  
For the first semester of fiscal year 2026, we received total allotment for the whole year of 2026, 15,773,503,000.

**[00:11:09.880] COA Assistant Commissioner Nilda B. Plaras** (asr-000116)  
And we were able to obligate 8,054,240,000 or 51.06%.

**[00:11:21.100] COA Assistant Commissioner Nilda B. Plaras** (asr-000117)  
Then we were able to disburse also 7,848,598,000 or 97.45% disbursement rate.

**[00:11:38.260] COA Assistant Commissioner Nilda B. Plaras** (asr-000118)  
Then we have the balance of unobligated allotment of 7,719,263,000.

**[00:11:51.100] COA Assistant Commissioner Nilda B. Plaras** (asr-000121)  
With unpaid obligations of 205,642,000.

taon hanggang ngayon ito po lagu yung top of mind issue when people think of

**[00:20:25.280] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000205)  
government.

**[00:20:26.600] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000206)  
At ito po ay korupsyon sa issue ng flood control, hot facilities at iba pang infrastructure projects.

**[00:20:33.520] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000207)  
Sa totoo lamang po, COWA dapat yung tatampal sa kamay ng korap at magsasabi na, huy, bawal yan.

**[00:20:41.960] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000208)  
Pero sabi ng COWA last year, dalawa lang daw ang taga-audit natin ng flood control.

**[00:20:48.340] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000209)  
Ang ending na ibulsa ang triliyong-triliyong pisong pera ng taong bayan.

**[00:20:54.380] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000210)  
Ang tanong po ay, natuganan na po ba natin?

**[00:20:57.680] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000211)  
Natuganan po ba sa kasulukuyang budget yung gap na ito?

**[00:21:01.880] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000212)  
At sa ngayon po, ilan na po ba ang nahire from last year to this year, Mr. Chairperson?

**[00:21:08.000] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000213)  
Thank you very much, Kong Sendanya, for that question.

**[00:21:11.700] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000214)  
Tungkol po muna doon sa binanggit ninyo, doon po sa, I think you were referring to the 1st District Engineering Office of Bulacan.

**[00:21:21.840] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000215)  
Ang ginawa po namin, galing na po sa Central Office ang naging tugon po namin through the Fraud Audit Office.

**[00:21:29.300] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000216)  
So, ang ginawa po namin ay nag-conduct na po kami ng Fraud Audit on marami na pong take-up na transaction po doon sa Bulacan.

**[00:21:39.460] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000217)  
Actually, I think we're able to have around 50 Fraud Audit, or 50 transactions.

**[00:21:46.860] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000218)  
And ito nga po ang ginagamit na po ng ombudsman.

**[00:21:52.000] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000219)  
Una pong muna, nung wala po, nung una po ay sinabit po namin ito sa ICI.

**[00:21:56.520] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000220)  
But noon pong natapos na po si ICI.

**[00:21:59.200] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000221)  
We submitted everything to ombudsman.

**[00:22:00.680] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000222)  
Ito na po ang ginagamit ng ombudsman for the cases that are being tried in the preliminary investigation po sa kanila,

**[00:22:09.360] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000223)  
and then sa Sandigan Bayan na po yung iba.

**[00:22:11.480] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000224)  
And also, our auditors are the ones, mga naging witnesses po dito.

**[00:22:17.600] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000225)  
Tungkol naman po doon sa pag-hire, I think natugunan naman po kami ng DBM dahil humingi po kami ng tulong.

**[00:22:24.720] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000226)  
Sabi po namin, dati po kasi kulang sa Region 3.

**[00:22:28.800] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000227)  
At saka po sa BARM, ang number of personnel, kasi po ay nabawasan po yun.

**[00:22:36.260] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000228)  
So, nandito po si ASCOM Lord na Kabutyan para po explain po yung grinan po ng DBM sa amin, na number of personnel.

**[00:22:44.360] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000229)  
Salamat po.

**[00:22:46.100] COA Assistant Commissioner Lorna Cabochan** (asr-000230)  
Good afternoon po.

**[00:22:48.600] COA Assistant Commissioner Lorna Cabochan** (asr-000231)  
As early as 2020, when our OSSP po, yung plantilla possessions was approved,

**[00:22:56.880] COA Assistant Commissioner Lorna Cabochan** (asr-000232)  
upon review,

**[00:22:59.480] COA Assistant Commissioner Lorna Cabochan** (asr-000233)  
meron po kami nabawasan kami ng around 716 possessions.

**[00:23:04.160] COA Assistant Commissioner Lorna Cabochan** (asr-000234)  
That's why we filed our MR with the DBM.

**[00:23:08.960] COA Assistant Commissioner Lorna Cabochan** (asr-000235)  
And luckily po, this was favorably acted upon by the DBM last July po.

**[00:23:19.080] COA Assistant Commissioner Lorna Cabochan** (asr-000236)  
And they already granted or restored the 332 possessions po.

**[00:23:25.640] COA Assistant Commissioner Lorna Cabochan** (asr-000237)  
And basically po,

**[00:23:27.240] COA Assistant Commissioner Lorna Cabochan** (asr-000238)  
ang merami po dito, like what the Chair mentioned, yung COWA-BARM po of 141 possessions because that time,

**[00:23:36.980] COA Assistant Commissioner Lorna Cabochan** (asr-000239)  
this was deducted to our original plantilla.

**[00:23:41.420] COA Assistant Commissioner Lorna Cabochan** (asr-000240)  
And COWA Region 3 of 123 and the others naman po,

**[00:23:46.660] COA Assistant Commissioner Lorna Cabochan** (asr-000241)  
with the other offices na very minimal po.

**[00:23:49.860] COA Assistant Commissioner Lorna Cabochan** (asr-000242)  
So we are very thankful po for the DBM in granting po yung MR namin.

**[00:23:55.640] COA Assistant Commissioner Lorna Cabochan** (asr-000243)  
Out of 716, we were restored to 332 positions.

**[00:24:05.100] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000244)  
So ilan pa po ang kailangan natin?

**[00:24:08.080] COA Assistant Commissioner Lorna Cabochan** (asr-000245)  
So the balance po ng 716, less 332 po is around mga almost 400 pa po.

**[00:24:18.260] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000246)  
So more than half pa yung kailangan natin from the original.

**[00:24:23.340] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000247)  
Is this a cause for concern?

**[00:24:26.700] COA Assistant Commissioner Lorna Cabochan** (asr-000248)  
Yes po, it's a cause for concern.

**[00:24:28.900] COA Assistant Commissioner Lorna Cabochan** (asr-000249)  
Because as the agencies are adding and as present,

**[00:24:37.200] COA Assistant Commissioner Lorna Cabochan** (asr-000250)  
medyo kulang na rin ang mga auditors natin in the field.

**[00:24:41.700] COA Assistant Commissioner Lorna Cabochan** (asr-000251)  
And aside from yung separation, meron din po kaming data on the separation of our auditors

**[00:24:51.460] COA Assistant Commissioner Lorna Cabochan** (asr-000252)  
less than 10 years who were staying with us.

**[00:24:56.300] COA Assistant Commissioner Lorna Cabochan** (asr-000253)  
During the exit interview, mas preferable po sa kanila yung higher pay

**[00:25:04.660] COA Assistant Commissioner Lorna Cabochan** (asr-000254)  
and proximity po sa residence nila and yung flexible working arrangement po.

**[00:25:11.580] COA Assistant Commissioner Lorna Cabochan** (asr-000255)  
So, though we are continually recruiting, meron din po kasing separation through transfer

**[00:25:23.660] COA Assistant Commissioner Lorna Cabochan** (asr-000256)  
po sa ibang government agencies and resignation.

**[00:25:27.750] COA Assistant Commissioner Lorna Cabochan** (asr-000257)  
And of course, also po ngayon, they are considering to retire at the age of 60 na po.

**[00:25:34.900] COA Assistant Commissioner Lorna Cabochan** (asr-000258)  
They wouldn't wait for 65 po. as soon as they reach the age of 60, they are availing the optional retirement.

**[00:25:45.880] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000262)  
Mr. Chair, sa totoo lamang po,

**[00:25:48.400] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000263)  
medyo alarming itong trend na ito.

**[00:25:50.900] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000264)  
Mas lalo ding alarming na kulang na kulang

**[00:25:53.320] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000265)  
pa rin pala hanggang ngayon.

**[00:25:55.540] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000266)  
Ngayong 2027 po, may proposed 107 billion pesos para sa flood control under DPWH.

**[00:26:03.360] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000267)  
Pagpalpak uli ang audit or kapag kulang, mabagal ang audit, repeat performance tayo sa 2025.

**[00:26:09.900] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000268)  
At alam na po natin kung ano yung dahilan. Ito nga po nabanggit natin nakakulangan.

**[00:26:14.200] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000269)  
on top of yung budgetary needs po natin, may plano ba ang COA na i-enhance ang capacity ng special audit teams

**[00:26:21.240] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000270)  
para maimbestigahan kung may gross and anomalous flood control projects ulit.

**[00:26:27.840] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-000271)  
Si Commissioner Douglas Malilin po ang magsasakit.

**[00:26:34.120] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000272)  
Magandang hapon po. May mga reforma po na ginagawa po ang COA.

**[00:26:40.720] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000273)  
Habang may kakulangan po ang aming budget lalo na ho sa capital outlay at may kakulangan din ho sa tao,

**[00:26:47.700] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000274)  
may mga ginagawa ho kaming pamamaraan para mapabilis naman ho yung natututunan ng mga tao.

**[00:26:56.440] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000275)  
Sa ngayon po, sa kabuuan ng lahat po ng regions namin, sinasalang ho namin sila sa mga mabibigat hon training courses

**[00:27:05.580] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000276)  
for data analytics po. Ito lang po ang pamamaraan ho namin ngayon dahil ang traditional ng audit ho

**[00:27:14.520] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000277)  
ay madalas alam namin na dinadaan ho sa papel. But with the present technology that we have

**[00:27:21.560] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000278)  
and with what we have in the basic technology, we have the ability to do that.

**[00:27:32.240] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000279)  
At the same time po, tinitingnan niyo namin yung mga ahensya na mapabago ho namin yung sistema.

**[00:27:40.740] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000280)  
Kung mamarapatin po namin ang accounting processes ho ng COA na isiniwalat ho namin sa lahat ng ahensya

**[00:27:49.140] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000281)  
ay nasa salubo ng kinatawag ho namin government accounting manual.

**[00:27:53.160] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000282)  
Kung titignan ho natin itong government accounting manual, may kalumaan na ho, bago ho yung issuance noong 2015.

**[00:28:00.760] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000283)  
Pero yung mga proseso ho ng COA, inaamin ho namin marami na ho kailangan palitan.

**[00:28:07.420] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000284)  
Napaparami ho yung papel.

**[00:28:09.120] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000285)  
Naiiba naman ho yung tawag ng teknolohiya. So binabago po namin yun.

**[00:28:15.780] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000286)  
So simula proseso ho hanggang kung anuman ng teknolohiya ang pwede namin gamitin ay ginagamit po namin ngayon.

**[00:28:23.440] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000287)  
Meron ho kaming iminungkahi noong nakaraang taon but because of the budget constraints,

**[00:28:33.220] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000288)  
ang ginawa ho namin muna ay kung ano lang muna magagawa in terms of what technology we can use.

**[00:28:39.580] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000289)  
At the same time po, meron nilabas po ang COA na resolution at circular po na geotagging.

**[00:28:46.600] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000290)  
Nakita ho namin na malaking bagay ho ito sa mga infrastructure projects.

**[00:28:52.800] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000291)  
We are coordinating po with DPWH so that yung geotagging requirements and our other stricter requirements po ay hindi naman po makasagabal sa kailangan ho nilang magampanan na proyekto.

**[00:29:06.560] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000292)  
Dahil pag sumobra naman po ang nagiging requirements at bumagal po yung pangangailangan, binabalansi ho namin ito.

**[00:29:15.600] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000293)  
Noong nakaraang taon, there was also a suggestion for pre-audit.

**[00:29:21.420] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000294)  
May kaakibat na budget din po ito pero ang ginagawa namin in the middle ground.

**[00:29:29.660] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000295)  
We are looking at ways and means po, para lang magkaroon ho ng continuous audit without exactly it being a pre-audit.

**[00:29:38.120] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000296)  
Marami hong mga programa ngayon sa loob ng COA.

**[00:29:41.540] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000297)  
Ang tinututukan namin ngayon muna po ay ang kaalaman ng mga tao to upskill them.

**[00:29:47.200] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000298)  
Dahil

**[00:29:48.180] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000299)  
Kung kulang kami sa tao at madalas nawawalan, di po kami ng tao.

**[00:29:52.900] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000300)  
We take a look at technology as the response po.

**[00:29:57.220] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000301)  
Maraming salamat po.

**[00:29:58.820] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000302)  
Mr. Chair, napakabait po ng pagka-frame ng ating COA Commissioner nung sinabi niya na kulang yung kanilang capital outlay.

**[00:30:05.860] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000303)  
Pero pag binilikan po natin yung budget pa, zero po ang capital outlay ng COA.

**[00:30:10.880] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000304)  
Ano po ang impact nitong zero capital outlay sa pag-acquire natin ng necessary technologies para mas paigihin po ang ating trabaho sa loob ng COA?

**[00:30:22.460] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000305)  
Meron po kaming, yung pamamaraan po namin ngayon po ay turuan po ng mga tauhan po namin ng tamang paggamit po ng teknolohiya.

**[00:30:32.600] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000306)  
We basically have two items po that we have been doing since last year.

**[00:30:38.780] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000307)  
Data analytics, at the same time po we're taking a look at enhancing po yung citizens participatory audit.

**[00:30:48.380] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000308)  
So kung kulang man po ang tao, pero matatapos.

**[00:30:51.880] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000309)  
At po namin ang publiko, mga NGOs, mga CSOs po, na tulungan po kami sa audit, nakakadagdag manpower din po ito.

**[00:31:00.360] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000310)  
Yun nga lang po, kasama po sa hiling namin na budget po for this program, yung citizens participatory audit, mukhang may kakulangan din po sa larangan po nung budget for that aspect po.

**[00:31:15.320] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000311)  
So overall po, despite the lack of a capital outlay, we do what we can.

**[00:31:22.560] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000312)  
Pero mas maganda po kung magpagbigyan po kami sa area po ng dagdag capital outlay po, dagdag opisina, dagdag teknolohiya, hardware and software po.

**[00:31:33.820] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000313)  
Mas maganda po ang magiging performance po ng audit dahil mag-iiba po ang landscape of how we audit.

**[00:31:42.100] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000314)  
Hindi na po yung papel po ang inahanap po namin.

**[00:31:45.440] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000315)  
Ang nangyayari po is magiging mas analytic po ang mga auditors po natin.

**[00:31:50.200] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000316)  
Maraming salamat po.

**[00:31:51.580] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000317)  
Again po, nakababahala itong zero capital outlay para sa COA, lalo na sa panahon kailangan-kailangan natin i-bolster yung capacity ng ahensya, lalo na pagdating sa software at hardware.

**[00:32:02.200] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000318)  
The only way for us to be more efficient is with efficient use of technology at hindi po yan hinuhugot sa hangin, kailangan po ang capital outlay.

**[00:32:09.700] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000319)  
And we hope that the committee will really work with COA in restoring the capital outlay proposal of COA, lalo na po sa panahon ito na matinding korupsyon.

**[00:32:20.480] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000320)  
Nabagid nyo po kanina.

**[00:32:22.140] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000321)  
Mayroong pre-audit systems. Ano po ba ang kailangan nating budgetary support para po sa staffing, training at implementation ng ating pre-audit system?

**[00:32:35.700] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000322)  
Mr. Chair, meron po kaming, ito po yung tinake up na rin po natin last year based on the question of Congressman Romeo Momo.

**[00:32:48.060] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000323)  
We have computation kasama po ang salaries, etc.

**[00:32:55.760] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000324)  
Lahat po ng kailangan.

**[00:32:56.780] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000325)  
We will have 146 additional pre-audit teams composed of various audit technical and administrative positions.

**[00:33:07.080] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000326)  
We can submit it po to the committee.

**[00:33:09.720] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000327)  
Lahat-lahat na po ito, more or less around 2.5 billion po ito.

**[00:33:13.780] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000328)  
Pero sa tingin po namin, sulit po ito dahil bigyan ko po kayong statistics just for this year, your honors, sa DPWH lang po ang na-issue po na notice,

**[00:33:24.260] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000329)  
ang notice of suspension na na-issue ng ating mga auditors sa DPWH is already 107.771 billion.

**[00:33:36.140] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000330)  
At ang notice of disallowance na na-issue po is 51.27 billion.

**[00:33:42.420] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000331)  
Of course, iba-iba na po ang stages ito ngayon dahil may appeal, etc.

**[00:33:46.240] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000332)  
But the total amount that was issued for DPWH only for this year is already 158 billion pesos.

**[00:33:54.460] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000333)  
Can you imagine po?

**[00:33:55.600] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000334)  
Ang magagagawa natin kung madadagdagan po ng 148 pre-audit teams.

**[00:34:02.020] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000335)  
Tama ba? 148 pre-audit teams ang ating Commission on Audit.

**[00:34:08.940] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000336)  
Napakalaking bagay po. Dito pa lang po sa existing natin ngayon, we already were able to do actions on 158 billion pesos.

**[00:34:18.900] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000337)  
Thank you.

**[00:34:20.020] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000338)  
Marami pong salamat and we support you on this call.

**[00:34:22.860] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000339)  
Lalo na po, napakahalaga ng pre-audit teams.

**[00:34:25.720] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000340)  
Kayo po talaga ang frontliners at first line of defense against corruption.

**[00:34:29.780] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000341)  
Kaya nga po, hindi ko maintindihan na while the government is saying na ang priority ay anti-corruption,

**[00:34:34.920] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000342)  
hindi po ba dapat binibigay natin ng dagdag na sandata, dagdag na resources at investment ang COA.

**[00:34:42.000] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000343)  
Kaya po, medyo I still find it quite difficult to believe na zero ang nilagay sa ating capital outlay

**[00:34:48.040] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000344)  
at ngayon ay bitin-bitin pa rin tayo padating po sa mga needs natin in terms of personnel.

**[00:34:52.700] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000345)  
Let me just go back to the issue of flood control.

**[00:34:56.700] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000346)  
Meron po ba tayong audit reforms na in-implement para pigilan ang license, renting schemes at contractor monopolies?

**[00:35:10.320] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000347)  
Para sagutin ko po yung tanong po ninyo.

**[00:35:14.360] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000348)  
Nung nagsimula po nagkaroon ng kontrobersiya po ang PICAB,

**[00:35:18.390] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000349)  
ang mga auditors po namin in the auditing PICAB po ay tumutok na po.

**[00:35:24.070] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000350)  
Ang unahong tinignan nila dahil dito po nang gagaling ang...

**[00:35:28.960] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000351)  
Yung malaking parte ng problema po ng PICAB ay ang qualifications po at yung conflict of...

**[00:35:34.120] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000352)  
possible conflict of interest.

**[00:35:36.280] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000353)  
Dito po unang nagkaroon ng problema.

**[00:35:39.280] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000354)  
But more so po, ang tinignan po namin ay ang kadikit po niyang mga sistema po sa loob ng DPWH.

**[00:35:47.200] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000355)  
Dahil po, inaayos na rin po ng DPWH yung mga sistema nila.

**[00:35:51.580] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000356)  
We are coordinating po with them.

**[00:35:53.960] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000357)  
So we're taking a look at all their data po.

**[00:35:56.680] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000358)  
So that moving forward...

**[00:35:58.960] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000359)  
Nakikita po natin, magkakaroon na po tayo ng idea kung sino ho yung mga contractor na medyo malalaki ho yung proyekto.

**[00:36:06.580] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000360)  
Sino yung nakakakuha sa bawat area.

**[00:36:10.340] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000361)  
So ang nangyayari po dito is we can do reverse engineering po.

**[00:36:15.430] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000362)  
Kung ano ho yung nakikita natin nakakakuha po ng kontrata o sumasali po sa bidding,

**[00:36:20.300] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000363)  
nakikita po natin we tie it back po sa PICAB data.

**[00:36:24.900] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000364)  
Nung una po, pag ina-audit namin ng PICAB, it is more of a...

**[00:36:29.920] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000365)  
But because of what happened po, pinapasok namin ho yung ibang modes of audit namin.

**[00:36:35.600] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000366)  
Compliance, performance audit, at meron pang iba pa.

**[00:36:39.020] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000367)  
Kaya ang pinapalakas po namin yung areas of weaknesses, lalo na po at this point in time where we saw the deficiencies po.

**[00:36:47.560] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000368)  
At naging red flag po sa COA at pinalakas din po namin yung sa side po namin ng COA kung ano po dapat namin gawin.

**[00:36:56.140] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000369)  
Salamat po.

**[00:36:57.880] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000370)  
Maraming salamat.

**[00:36:59.420] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000371)  
Mr. Chair, may the committee kindly direct the Commission on Audit to submit a progress report on these efforts pertaining to license renting and contractor monopolies.

**[00:37:09.920] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000372)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[00:37:10.820] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000373)  
And for my last set of questions, simple lamang po.

**[00:37:16.400] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000374)  
Napakahalaga po talaga ng AAR, no po?

**[00:37:19.260] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000375)  
At nabanggit po ninyo na ang deadline ng annual audit reports ay September 2026.

**[00:37:26.520] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000376)  
September na po next week, no?

**[00:37:28.300] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000377)  
May we ask the COA kung ilang percent na po ng mga agencies ang nakapag-submit ng kanilang AARs for 2025 at ilan pa po ang hinihintayin natin?

**[00:37:37.060] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000378)  
Natanong ko po ito kasi inaabangan po talaga namin sa inyong website yung publication ng mga AARs.

**[00:37:46.320] COA Assistant Commissioner Nilda B. Plaras** (asr-000379)  
For

**[00:37:47.160] COA Assistant Commissioner Nilda B. Plaras** (asr-000380)  
the annual audit reports, the deadline of the transmittal to the audited agency was June 30.

**[00:37:58.080] COA Assistant Commissioner Nilda B. Plaras** (asr-000381)  
Then we have 60 days from the...

**[00:38:00.420] COA Assistant Commissioner Nilda B. Plaras** (asr-000382)  
Transmittal for requiring the audited agency to submit the actions taken on the audit observations and recommendations.

**[00:38:11.280] COA Assistant Commissioner Nilda B. Plaras** (asr-000383)  
Then these actions taken by the management will be appended to the annual audit report to be published in the COA website.

**[00:38:23.640] COA Assistant Commissioner Nilda B. Plaras** (asr-000384)  
So we are given until October 31 to publish the annual audit reports.

**[00:38:30.760] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000385)  
Ibig sabihin po, nakasabing na po ang lahat ng mga ahensya o ilang percent na po ang nakasabing?

**[00:38:35.140] COA Assistant Commissioner Nilda B. Plaras** (asr-000386)  
No, transmitted to the audited agencies.

**[00:38:37.820] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000387)  
To the audited agencies. Okay. Sige po. Marami pong salamat.

**[00:38:41.280] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000388)  
Siguro po as a last point, we say that anti-corruption is a priority of the government and yet we do not see this in the allocation that was given to COA.

**[00:38:53.440] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000389)  
Kung sinasabi po natin na ang budget ang pinakakongkreta expression of the priorities of government,

**[00:38:59.480] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000390)  
then it seems like...

**[00:39:00.740] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000391)  
Based on this budget, particularly yung nakababahalang zero capital outlay sa panahon na kailangan-kailangan natin ng teknolihiya ay hindi binigay sa COA.

**[00:39:09.640] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000392)  
So again, we question if truly anti-corruption is the priority if it's not reflective of yung investments na niligay sa napakahalagang ahensya ng ito.

**[00:39:20.060] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000393)  
Muli po kayo ang ating frontliners at first time defense against corruption at sana mabigyan kayo ng sapat na support ng gobyerno.

**[00:39:28.060] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000394)  
Mabuhay po kayo. Salamat po, Mr. Chair.

**[00:39:32.360] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-000395)  
Thank you, Representative Cendaña.

**[00:39:34.800] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-000396)  
We now call from the majority, Representative Mauricio Domogan, sir.

**[00:39:44.560] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-000397)  
Thank you, Mr. Chairman.

**[00:39:47.020] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-000398)  
Good afternoon, the COA family, leaded by our chairperson, Apo Cordoba.

**[00:39:56.480] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-000399)  
I was the one who brought last year.

**[00:40:03.400] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-000400)  
Siguro walang nanakaw kung hindi natin inalis yung pre-audit.

**[00:40:08.780] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-000401)  
Umuon naman kayo last year to that effect.

**[00:40:12.260] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-000402)  
Yung problema lang, sabi ninyo, kulang yung manpower.

**[00:40:19.080] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-000403)  
Relative to the question of Congressman Zindania,

**[00:40:23.180] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-000404)  
bakit hindi natin binigyan ng priority?

**[00:40:27.580] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-000405)  
Yung increase sa personal...

**[00:40:31.540] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-000406)  
services.

**[00:40:33.540] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-000407)  
So that we hire additional auditors that will be conducting pre-audit.

**[00:40:43.140] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-000408)  
Actually, I filed already a bill pursuant to what we had discussed last year.

**[00:40:51.840] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-000409)  
Making pre-audit coupled with geo-mapping

**[00:40:58.080] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-000410)  
as mandatory requisite of

**[00:41:02.360] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-000411)  
payment of government projects.

**[00:41:07.980] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-000412)  
Kasi kung ginawa natin ito,

**[00:41:12.240] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-000413)  
imposible na may ghost project na nababayaran.

**[00:41:18.060] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-000414)  
As we all know, post-audit usually concentrate on the record submitted.

**[00:41:23.660] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-000415)  
Di masyado tinitingnan yung project. O, nakakalusot na.

**[00:41:28.820] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-000416)  
So I hope that COA,

**[00:41:32.220] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-000417)  
should support this bill.

**[00:41:36.820] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-000418)  
And my question now is,

**[00:41:40.400] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-000419)  
do we have an amount that we need

**[00:41:44.600] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-000420)  
to augment yung inyong budget

**[00:41:47.840] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-000421)  
for 2027

**[00:41:50.560] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-000422)  
in order to hire the necessary personnels

**[00:41:56.700] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-000423)  
to perform pre-audit

**[00:42:00.940] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-000424)  
coupled with

**[00:42:02.380] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-000426)  
geo-mapping?

**[00:42:07.800] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-000427)  
Maraming salamat po, Apo.

**[00:42:10.420] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000428)  
First of all po,

**[00:42:11.440] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000429)  
we manifest our support for your pending bill.

**[00:42:17.440] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000430)  
Kung kailangan po kaming mag-attend

**[00:42:19.060] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000431)  
or maging resource person po sa inyong pending bill,

**[00:42:23.060] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000432)  
we are ready to help po doon.

**[00:42:26.060] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000433)  
Secondly po, tungkol po dun sa amount that you mentioned,

**[00:42:31.900] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000434)  
we already submitted na rin po ito.

**[00:42:33.980] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000435)  
Last year, but we will resubmit it again.

**[00:42:35.960] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000436)  
At meron na po kami.

**[00:42:37.860] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000437)  
Tulad po ng nabanggit ko kanina,

**[00:42:39.560] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000438)  
we would need additional 146 pre-audit teams

**[00:42:43.420] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000439)  
composed of several personnel per team.

**[00:42:47.060] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000440)  
And nandito na po yung whole amount.

**[00:42:49.660] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000441)  
Our estimate is around 2.2 billion po for pre-audit.

**[00:42:55.560] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000442)  
But 2.2 billion pesos.

**[00:42:58.520] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000443)  
But actually po, as I have mentioned earlier,

**[00:43:00.540] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000444)  
napaka maliit po ito

**[00:43:02.440] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000445)  
kumpara po sa magiging action.

**[00:43:04.660] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000446)  
If we have this, kapalis po nang sinabi ko kanina,

**[00:43:07.260] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000447)  
even without this,

**[00:43:09.420] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000448)  
ang na-issue po na NS,

**[00:43:11.640] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000449)  
or Notice of Suspension ng aming DPWH team,

**[00:43:16.340] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000450)  
ay 107 billion na po agad.

**[00:43:19.240] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000451)  
So kung titigan niyo po,

**[00:43:20.660] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000452)  
yung butal pa lang po noon ay katapat na po nito.

**[00:43:23.640] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000453)  
And then meron pa pong NDNCs

**[00:43:26.460] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000454)  
na na-issue na 51 of total of 158 billion.

**[00:43:31.060] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000455)  
Doon pa lang po sa butal ay ito pong gastos na to.

**[00:43:34.320] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000456)  
So sa 2.1 ay kuwang-kuwa na po, Your Honors.

**[00:43:36.780] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000457)  
Salamat po.

**[00:43:38.640] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000459)  
Chairman, thank you.

**[00:43:40.400] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-000460)  
Presiding Officer.

**[00:43:43.940] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-000461)  
I am sure not only me,

**[00:43:48.640] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-000462)  
but my colleagues will support

**[00:43:50.880] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-000463)  
the motion later on in the floor.

**[00:43:54.320] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-000464)  
Nadagdagan yung inyong budget to comply with this one.

**[00:43:57.240] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-000465)  
If we are really serious to fight

**[00:43:59.600] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-000466)  
corruption,

**[00:44:04.380] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-000467)  
pinapakinggan ko yung presentation ninyo eh,

**[00:44:06.480] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-000468)  
may additional personnel.

**[00:44:08.120] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-000469)  
Ngunit,

**[00:44:08.860] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-000470)  
hindi nakatutok sa talagang requirement.

**[00:44:13.880] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-000471)  
Parang ito nga,

**[00:44:14.840] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-000472)  
mapigil itong ano.

**[00:44:16.800] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-000473)  
Imagine,

**[00:44:17.480] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-000474)  
aabot ng halos billion-billions yung nawala.

**[00:44:22.920] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-000475)  
Kasi,

**[00:44:23.340] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-000476)  
if you look at what happened,

**[00:44:26.580] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-000477)  
pre-audit was being done.

**[00:44:28.760] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-000478)  
It was only removed in 2011.

**[00:44:33.220] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-000479)  
That was,

**[00:44:33.900] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-000480)  
what happened.

**[00:44:36.140] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-000481)  
And eh,

**[00:44:39.040] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-000482)  
yung talagang ghost projects na nababayaran,

**[00:44:43.100] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-000483)  
yun na nangyari.

**[00:44:45.160] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-000484)  
See,

**[00:44:45.920] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-000485)  
I remember the rehabilitation of Baguio

**[00:44:48.380] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-000486)  
from the ruins of the killer quick.

**[00:44:50.740] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-000487)  
And I am happy that we have some

**[00:44:52.360] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-000488)  
graduates of Baguio here with the commission.

**[00:44:57.460] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-000489)  
A lot of you.

**[00:44:58.140] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-000490)  
A lot of you, even the one who testified in the impeachment is from Erisan Bagusitian, graduate of St. Louis University.

**[00:45:08.280] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-000491)  
Eh, siyong ko siguro may balik natin yan, malaking bagay sa ating gobyerno if again we are serious of addressing this issue of corruption.

**[00:45:21.780] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-000492)  
Eh, hindi siguro lulusot yung ano. Kasi kung may pre-audit coupled with geo-tagging or mapping, talagang maliwanag na nandyan na yung project.

**[00:45:36.880] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-000493)  
Eh, hindi yung ghost projects pagkatapos full payment. Ano naman yan?

**[00:45:44.120] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-000494)  
Eh, yung talagang maliwanag na nangyayari, pinapayagan natin yung magnanakaw na.

**[00:45:51.500] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-000495)  
Magnanakaw ka muna bago namin kayo kabulin. Asan tayo ngayon? Mahabol mo pa ng nasa Amerika.

**[00:45:58.220] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-000496)  
Hindi pa natin alam kung asan siya at this point in time.

**[00:46:04.100] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-000497)  
So, thank you for your manifestation to support this one.

**[00:46:10.620] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-000498)  
Eh, well, I was about to ask, alright, the result of your audit na pinakita ninyo, ilan yan yung na-issue ng AOM at Notice of Order?

**[00:46:22.800] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000500)  
Yun pong sa nangyayari po dun sa DPWH sa Bulacan, we're able to have fraud audit po sa 50 projects. Lahat po yun ay na-issuean po namin.

**[00:46:35.280] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-000501)  
Yeah, but for this year, 2026.

**[00:46:37.800] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000502)  
Ah, okay. Ito po. Ang na-issuean po namin ng NS na po, Notice of Suspension of Payment is 107 billion. Sa DPWH pa lang po ito.

**[00:46:46.580] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000503)  
Sa ND, or Notice of Charge po, tsaka Notice of Disallowance, nasa 50.

**[00:46:50.740] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000504)  
51 billion po. For a total of 158 billion.

**[00:46:54.990] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-000505)  
Na yun mga naman, nabayaran na.

**[00:46:58.220] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000506)  
Ah, yun po, nabayaran na po.

**[00:46:59.660] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-000507)  
Ayun na, yung problema.

**[00:47:01.680] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-000509)  
Kung siguro pre-audit, hindi nabayaran yan.

**[00:47:04.560] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-000511)  
Tingnan mo yan, billions pa.

**[00:47:08.460] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-000513)  
That's why, talagang kailangan na kailangan ito.

**[00:47:14.120] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000514)  
Ah, meron lang po idadagdag, ah, ah po.

**[00:47:16.380] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-000515)  
Yes, ah.

**[00:47:16.980] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000516)  
Yun pong binanggit nyo kanina, na pre-audit with geo-tagging.

**[00:47:20.840] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000517)  
Nakalaking bagay po nung word na geo-tagging.

**[00:47:22.500] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000518)  
Dahil dito po sa fraud audit namin, medyo napapansin po namin,

**[00:47:27.380] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000519)  
nahirapan po kaming hanapin yun mismo pong project.

**[00:47:30.620] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000520)  
We don't know if it's really ghost or not.

**[00:47:32.960] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000521)  
Because iba-iba po yung coordinates.

**[00:47:35.880] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-000522)  
Correct.

**[00:47:37.200] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000523)  
Yung po ang aming, kaya po nahirapan din po ang aming auditor na maghanap.

**[00:47:39.980] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000524)  
Itinuturo-turo lang po sila.

**[00:47:41.420] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000525)  
But yung po ang coordinates, nagbabago po kasi.

**[00:47:44.540] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-000526)  
Yeah.

**[00:47:45.790] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-000528)  
So, importante din yung geo-tagging.

**[00:47:51.360] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-000529)  
Yep, Mr. Chairman, thank you very much.

**[00:47:53.540] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-000530)  
Unfortunately, mag-expire na yung aking time.

**[00:47:57.020] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-000531)  
May second round pa naman.

**[00:47:58.440] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-000532)  
Thank you, ah, COA family.

**[00:48:00.000] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-000533)  
Ah, thank you, Representative Mauricio Dumogan, sir.

**[00:48:05.420] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-000534)  
May we proceed with the minority.

**[00:48:08.380] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-000535)  
Representative Iris Montes, ma'am.

**[00:48:11.980] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000536)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[00:48:13.540] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000537)  
Magandang hapon po sa COA, sa ating lahat.

**[00:48:17.760] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000538)  
Ah, let me begin by saying that alam naman po nating lahat

**[00:48:20.940] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000539)  
kung ano ang role namin.

**[00:48:21.780] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000540)  
Ah, napakahalaga ng role na ginagampanan ng COA,

**[00:48:25.080] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000541)  
especially during these times,

**[00:48:27.180] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000542)  
na talamak po talaga yung mga issues sa korupsyon.

**[00:48:31.100] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000543)  
I cannot overemphasize your role, ano po.

**[00:48:33.880] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000544)  
And then, dito din po sa Kongreso,

**[00:48:35.920] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000545)  
meron din po kaming oversight functions that we really have to do.

**[00:48:39.960] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000546)  
And bahagi po ng oversight function na yan,

**[00:48:42.480] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000547)  
ay, ah, nakaasa po kami sa COA

**[00:48:45.540] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000548)  
pagdating sa mga AAR, sa annual audit reports.

**[00:48:49.440] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000549)  
Because, um, I have been in, ah, before being a,

**[00:48:51.820] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000550)  
Congresswoman, I've been in Congress for the last three decades.

**[00:48:55.120] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000551)  
And I would always rely on the audit reports of COA

**[00:48:58.420] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000552)  
to be able to frame questions

**[00:49:00.580] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000553)  
so that my principal would be able to write,

**[00:49:03.060] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000554)  
to ask the right questions, ano.

**[00:49:06.860] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000555)  
So, um, itatanong ko po tungkol po,

**[00:49:08.660] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000556)  
can you walk us through very briefly

**[00:49:10.660] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000557)  
paano po ginagawa ang AARs?

**[00:49:14.020] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000558)  
Um, halimbawa po for 2026, itong 2026,

**[00:49:17.640] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000559)  
can you explain to us very briefly how to go about it?

**[00:49:29.380] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000560)  
Magandang

**[00:49:29.980] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000561)  
hapon po.

**[00:49:30.420] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000562)  
Um, mamaya po, ah, if you could, ah, give the floor po

**[00:49:35.600] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000563)  
to Assistant Commissioner Cese.

**[00:49:37.980] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000564)  
But, ah, I would like to give you po an overview

**[00:49:40.780] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000565)  
of, ah, the annual audit report.

**[00:49:43.660] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000566)  
Yung annual audit report po ay katumbas po

**[00:49:46.540] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000567)  
ng audited financial statements po

**[00:49:48.380] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000568)  
ng mga private companies.

**[00:49:50.680] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000569)  
Ang may, ah, nakabase po sa aming batas,

**[00:49:55.840] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000570)  
ah, Presidential Decree 1445,

**[00:49:58.730] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000571)  
ah, we require,

**[00:49:59.820] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000572)  
all the auditees po

**[00:50:01.540] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000573)  
to, ah, submit their trial balances

**[00:50:05.580] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000574)  
or their, um, their initial balance sheet

**[00:50:10.140] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000575)  
and income statement on February 14

**[00:50:12.480] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000576)  
of the following year.

**[00:50:14.810] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000577)  
Kaya po kung 2025 po,

**[00:50:17.080] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000578)  
they are required to meet the deadline

**[00:50:19.720] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000579)  
until February 14 of 2026.

**[00:50:24.840] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000580)  
Pagkasubmit po nila ng mga trial balances po nila,

**[00:50:27.880] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000581)  
um

**[00:50:29.820] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000582)  
ang pinakasimula po ng proseso

**[00:50:32.080] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000583)  
ng pag-audit po,

**[00:50:33.580] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000584)  
nagsisimula pa nga ho yan ng

**[00:50:35.480] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000585)  
bago 2026.

**[00:50:38.340] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000586)  
Meron ho kami mga prosesong tinatawag na

**[00:50:40.740] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000587)  
yung mga audit sector po namin,

**[00:50:43.400] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000588)  
nag-i-issue po ng mga general audit instructions,

**[00:50:46.540] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000589)  
part ho yan ng planning phase.

**[00:50:48.160] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000590)  
Then, susunod ho yan, bababa pa yan,

**[00:50:51.180] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000591)  
at magkakaroon po ng tinatawag namin

**[00:50:53.200] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000592)  
specific audit instructions, pababa.

**[00:50:56.020] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000593)  
But the actual work begins po

**[00:50:58.420] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000594)  
when we start scrutinizing,

**[00:51:00.240] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000595)  
the paperwork is when they start submitting

**[00:51:03.320] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000596)  
their trial balances.

**[00:51:04.500] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000597)  
So February 14.

**[00:51:06.760] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000598)  
Magkakaroon po kami ng mga tinatawag naming

**[00:51:09.060] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000599)  
entrance conferences.

**[00:51:10.820] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000600)  
So this is the time that

**[00:51:12.740] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000601)  
we start discussing po

**[00:51:14.940] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000602)  
with the auditee,

**[00:51:16.400] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000603)  
yung dating mga findings po ng COA

**[00:51:21.520] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000604)  
Kung ano po ang mga naresolva,

**[00:51:24.120] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000605)  
kung ano po yung bitin,

**[00:51:25.380] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000606)  
at kailangan po hong gawin.

**[00:51:27.760] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000607)  
So aside from the financials,

**[00:51:29.860] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000608)  
we also take a look at the audit findings

**[00:51:31.880] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000609)  
of the previous year and its resolution.

**[00:51:34.580] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000610)  
Then moving forward po,

**[00:51:36.320] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000611)  
yung mga audit teams that are assigned,

**[00:51:38.780] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000612)  
assuming po ito ay singular entity,

**[00:51:42.500] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000613)  
wala po siyang sabihin nating regional office,

**[00:51:46.010] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000614)  
so we only have one audit team

**[00:51:48.080] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000615)  
to take a look at this,

**[00:51:49.280] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000616)  
and they are given po a deadline

**[00:51:51.400] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000617)  
so that by June 30 po,

**[00:51:54.360] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000618)  
tapos na po yung kanilang annual audit report.

**[00:51:57.980] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000619)  
Ito po ay isang overview.

**[00:51:59.860] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000620)  
But for certain agencies po

**[00:52:02.820] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000621)  
that are multi-office,

**[00:52:04.960] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000622)  
tulad ho ng may mga regional offices,

**[00:52:07.340] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000623)  
tulad ho ng Department of Education,

**[00:52:09.980] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000624)  
na maraming ho silang district schools,

**[00:52:13.160] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000625)  
maraming din ho silang regional offices,

**[00:52:15.160] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000626)  
So meron ng proseso

**[00:52:17.400] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000627)  
sa ibang areas po,

**[00:52:19.360] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000628)  
na we start auditing from the ground,

**[00:52:23.240] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000629)  
may mga auditors po kami sa iba-ibang region,

**[00:52:26.560] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000630)  
they submit their audit reports,

**[00:52:28.780] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000631)  
to the central auditing team,

**[00:52:32.020] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000632)  
and the central auditing team po

**[00:52:34.420] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000633)  
will consolidate these reports.

**[00:52:36.020] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000634)  
So we have what we call

**[00:52:38.160] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000635)  
the consolidated annual audit reports.

**[00:52:42.200] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000636)  
So kung malaki ho yung ahensya,

**[00:52:44.080] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000637)  
baka magtaka po kayo,

**[00:52:46.100] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000638)  
kakaiba ang tawag ho namin ka-R,

**[00:52:47.840] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000639)  
dahil ho consolidated.

**[00:52:49.600] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000640)  
Yung mga ibang ahensya ho na singular entity,

**[00:52:52.260] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000641)  
AR lang po.

**[00:52:53.480] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000642)  
But they're all the same po.

**[00:52:56.280] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000643)  
So come June 30 po,

**[00:52:58.160] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000644)  
ito po ay natapos na po,

**[00:53:01.720] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000645)  
nakausap na po yung ahensya.

**[00:53:03.500] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000646)  
Then comes the reglamentary periods

**[00:53:06.400] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000647)  
that we observe,

**[00:53:07.720] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000648)  
yung tinatawag ho ni

**[00:53:08.860] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000649)  
Assistant Commissioner Nilda po,

**[00:53:11.360] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000650)  
na may 60 days.

**[00:53:13.580] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000651)  
Then, eventually po,

**[00:53:14.720] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000652)  
based on the General Appropriations Act,

**[00:53:17.840] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000653)  
that's the deadline we are given

**[00:53:20.360] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000654)  
to publish po sa COA report.

**[00:53:24.200] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000655)  
Although po, yung publication po namin kakaiba,

**[00:53:26.980] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000656)  
sa aming website,

**[00:53:28.500] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000657)  
may ibang kakaibang deadline naman po kami sa Kongreso.

**[00:53:33.500] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000658)  
Dahil po, nauuna rin po kayong nakakatanggap

**[00:53:37.360] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000659)  
ng mga audit reports na kinakailangan ho sa budget,

**[00:53:40.880] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000660)  
kakaiba naman po yung nilalagay ho namin sa website.

**[00:53:43.980] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000661)  
Magkapareho yung report.

**[00:53:45.020] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000662)  
Iba lang po yung panahon

**[00:53:46.580] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000663)  
ng aming pagpo-post po sa website.

**[00:53:49.540] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000664)  
In other words,

**[00:53:51.440] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000665)  
what is the turn-around time?

**[00:53:53.400] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000666)  
For instance, yung fiscal year 2024,

**[00:53:56.640] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000667)  
kailan po dapat nailabas yung Consolidated Audit Report?

**[00:54:02.200] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000668)  
Kung fiscal year 2024 po,

**[00:54:05.240] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000669)  
ito po ay lalabas ng June of 2025,

**[00:54:10.120] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000670)  
that is the deadline.

**[00:54:11.530] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000671)  
Then, mapapublish po ito,

**[00:54:13.380] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000672)  
base dun po sa requirements po

**[00:54:16.480] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000673)  
or instructions po ng GAA.

**[00:54:18.320] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000674)  
Which is?

**[00:54:20.460] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000675)  
That time po, it was December 31,

**[00:54:23.000] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000676)  
of 2025.

**[00:54:26.620] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000677)  
Pinaaga lang po ngayong 2026 po yung requirement po.

**[00:54:30.920] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000678)  
So, what is the latest AAR meron po kayo ngayon?

**[00:54:34.240] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000679)  
Nasa report po ninyo 2025 is already available?

**[00:54:39.220] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000680)  
Nasaan po yung 2025?

**[00:54:40.960] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000681)  
Nasa website because 10 minutes ago,

**[00:54:43.000] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000682)  
I checked the website, wala pa po doon.

**[00:54:45.540] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000683)  
Nasaan po siya?

**[00:54:46.520] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000684)  
Ang deadline po ngayong taon po ay October 31, 2025

**[00:54:49.700] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000685)  
for publication po sa website.

**[00:54:51.860] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000686)  
Pero transmitted na po sa mga ahensya

**[00:54:56.240] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000687)  
ng June 30 po yung kopya po nila.

**[00:54:58.340] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000688)  
So 2024 pa lang dapat talaga ang available by now?

**[00:55:03.220] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000689)  
Yes po. Tama po.

**[00:55:04.580] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000690)  
So, is there a way we can fast track it?

**[00:55:08.000] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000691)  
Ano po mag mga delays ang pwede nating ma-address

**[00:55:12.140] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000692)  
or mga challenges so that we can advance it a little bit

**[00:55:15.740] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000693)  
para pagdating dito sa Kongreso medyo updated po

**[00:55:18.420] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000694)  
yung fiscal year na ating pinagbabase?

**[00:55:24.240] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000695)  
If I could turn over the floor po sa isa sa mga assistant commissioners po namin

**[00:55:29.060] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000696)  
to give you an insight po as to what are the usual concerns

**[00:55:33.380] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000697)  
and what causes the bottlenecks po for the publication.

**[00:55:36.960] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-000698)  
Thank you po.

**[00:55:43.540] COA Assistant Commissioner Martha Roxana C. Sese** (asr-000699)  
Hello. Good afternoon po.

**[00:55:47.260] COA Assistant Commissioner Martha Roxana C. Sese** (asr-000700)  
Based on that overview po and looking at the legal deadlines po,

**[00:55:53.620] COA Assistant Commissioner Martha Roxana C. Sese** (asr-000701)  
actually we are on time naman po.

**[00:55:57.140] COA Assistant Commissioner Martha Roxana C. Sese** (asr-000702)  
In fact, if there are certain challenges po,

**[00:56:00.480] COA Assistant Commissioner Martha Roxana C. Sese** (asr-000703)  
like for example, an auditee is not able to submit the financial statements

**[00:56:05.360] COA Assistant Commissioner Martha Roxana C. Sese** (asr-000704)  
kasi dapat po all or nothing.

**[00:56:08.180] COA Assistant Commissioner Martha Roxana C. Sese** (asr-000705)  
So that's when we accept na kompleto yung financial statements.

**[00:56:12.600] COA Assistant Commissioner Martha Roxana C. Sese** (asr-000706)  
Kahit ganun pa man po yung situation,

**[00:56:14.840] COA Assistant Commissioner Martha Roxana C. Sese** (asr-000707)  
we still come up with an audit report minus the FS.

**[00:56:21.920] COA Assistant Commissioner Martha Roxana C. Sese** (asr-000708)  
It will follow po.

**[00:56:23.200] COA Assistant Commissioner Martha Roxana C. Sese** (asr-000709)  
So management letter po yun.

**[00:56:26.620] COA Assistant Commissioner Martha Roxana C. Sese** (asr-000710)  
In short po, namimit naman po namin lahat yung deadlines for the output.

**[00:56:32.000] COA Assistant Commissioner Martha Roxana C. Sese** (asr-000711)  
Henceforth po, this year and even last year, June 30,

**[00:56:37.120] COA Assistant Commissioner Martha Roxana C. Sese** (asr-000712)  
well delivered po lahat yung aming annual audit reports,

**[00:56:42.360] COA Assistant Commissioner Martha Roxana C. Sese** (asr-000713)  
consolidated annual audit reports,

**[00:56:44.820] COA Assistant Commissioner Martha Roxana C. Sese** (asr-000714)  
even the management letters po for the foreign assisted projects.

**[00:56:49.640] COA Assistant Commissioner Martha Roxana C. Sese** (asr-000715)  
Kasi may mga reports po.

**[00:56:51.160] COA Assistant Commissioner Martha Roxana C. Sese** (asr-000716)  
That started last year.

**[00:56:53.100] COA Assistant Commissioner Martha Roxana C. Sese** (asr-000717)  
Lahat po na-deliver namin.

**[00:56:54.660] COA Assistant Commissioner Martha Roxana C. Sese** (asr-000718)  
I think yung issue lang po would be kung kailan po ipapublish

**[00:57:01.100] COA Assistant Commissioner Martha Roxana C. Sese** (asr-000719)  
that will be for the public consumption po.

**[00:57:03.900] COA Assistant Commissioner Martha Roxana C. Sese** (asr-000720)  
Pero again, we were also able to deliver din po sa mga oversight bodies

**[00:57:09.720] COA Assistant Commissioner Martha Roxana C. Sese** (asr-000721)  
yung mga reports po right after the June 30 deadline po.

**[00:57:14.780] COA Assistant Commissioner Martha Roxana C. Sese** (asr-000722)  
And other than that din po, meron din po yung submissions namin sa mga committees,

**[00:57:20.640] COA Assistant Commissioner Martha Roxana C. Sese** (asr-000723)  
sa House of Representatives, sa Senate po.

**[00:57:25.400] COA Assistant Commissioner Martha Roxana C. Sese** (asr-000724)  
Those who require the reports for their studies,

**[01:57:23.320] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-002460)

**[01:57:55.880] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-002474)

**[01:58:26.240] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002486)  
Good afternoon.

**[01:58:29.180] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002487)  
To answer the questions

**[01:58:30.860] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002488)  
regarding the Citizen Participatory Audit,

**[01:58:35.240] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002489)  
may pagkakaiba po na

**[01:58:38.940] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002491)  
nagsulong po ng complaint

**[01:58:40.400] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002492)  
ang isang private citizen

**[01:58:42.220] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002493)  
regarding a certain project

**[01:58:44.580] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002494)  
or a certain transaction po.

**[01:58:47.160] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002495)  
Normally po,

**[01:58:48.260] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002496)  
if the complaint is submitted,

**[01:58:51.040] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002497)  
this would fall under our COA complaints desk,

**[01:58:54.220] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002498)  
and this would be transmitted to

**[01:58:57.480] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002499)  
the audit sector po

**[01:58:59.900] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002500)  
na sumasako po sa area na to.

**[01:59:03.420] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002501)  
Kung nanggagaling po ito sa Cebu,

**[01:59:06.060] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002502)  
nanggagaling po ito sa Apari,

**[01:59:09.280] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002503)  
kung sino po ang local government audit sector po

**[01:59:12.220] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002504)  
sa kanya po itatransmit to look into.

**[01:59:15.720] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002505)  
Kakaiba naman po yung

**[01:59:17.200] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002506)  
Citizens Participatory Audit.

**[01:59:19.460] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002507)  
Yung proseso na to,

**[01:59:20.760] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002508)  
although it does involve citizens po,

**[01:59:23.920] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002509)  
we have this program po

**[01:59:25.740] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002510)  
to have NGOs,

**[01:59:28.000] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002511)  
private citizens,

**[01:59:29.580] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002512)  
and CSOs po.

**[01:59:30.740] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002513)  
They become part po of the audit team.

**[01:59:34.340] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002514)  
Magkakaroon po ng isang programa na

**[01:59:36.600] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002515)  
kung kailangan ma-audit ang isang

**[01:59:38.820] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002516)  
infrastruktura o isang programa po.

**[01:59:42.160] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002517)  
Nagiging parte po sila ng audit team.

**[01:59:44.240] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002518)  
They are trained po how to audit.

**[01:59:46.780] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002519)  
So they become part of the audit team complement.

**[01:59:51.840] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002520)  
Nagiging very formal po yung process.

**[01:59:54.480] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002521)  
They are trained

**[01:59:55.120] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002522)  
how to go about it.

**[01:59:57.960] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002523)  
Tinuturuan ho sila kung ano

**[01:59:59.160] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002524)  
ho dapat tingnan.

**[02:00:02.060] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002525)  
Inaayos ho din sila

**[02:00:02.900] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002526)  
na tingnan at makagawa po

**[02:00:05.320] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002527)  
ng report tulad ho ng mga

**[02:00:07.320] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002528)  
auditor at ginagabayan ho sila

**[02:00:09.300] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002529)  
ng COA. Ito ho

**[02:00:11.420] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002530)  
ay isinasama ho namin sila sa

**[02:00:13.180] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002531)  
proseso dahil gusto

**[02:00:15.340] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002532)  
rin na ho namin as COA

**[02:00:17.140] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002533)  
na makita ng pribadong

**[02:00:19.100] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002534)  
sektor kung ano ho ang ginagawa

**[02:00:21.440] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002535)  
ng COA. At the same time

**[02:00:23.280] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002536)  
po dahil very voluntary po yung

**[02:00:25.400] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002537)  
participation nila

**[02:00:27.400] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002538)  
although COA ho shoulders

**[02:00:28.520] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002539)  
part of their expenses, it

**[02:00:31.140] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002540)  
becomes a more effective audit

**[02:00:33.340] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002541)  
po. So kakaiba po yung

**[02:00:35.040] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002542)  
ordinary complaint where

**[02:00:36.540] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002543)  
a citizen based on the

**[02:00:39.080] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002544)  
observation or based on a complaint

**[02:00:41.990] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002545)  
simply submits

**[02:00:42.900] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002546)  
what they think COA

**[02:00:45.080] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002547)  
should look into. Yung

**[02:00:46.880] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002548)  
kinatawag ko nating CPA, Citizens

**[02:00:49.040] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002549)  
Participatory Audit,

**[02:00:50.700] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002550)  
lumalabas ho, they do not

**[02:00:52.800] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002551)  
just do one act.

**[02:00:55.780] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002552)  
Nasasama ho sila

**[02:00:56.880] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002553)  
sa buong gawain ng pag-audit.

**[02:00:59.120] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002554)  
And we value

**[02:01:00.840] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002555)  
po the output

**[02:01:02.960] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002556)  
and the outcomes of their audit report.

**[02:01:05.380] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002557)  
So yung programa

**[02:01:07.120] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002558)  
po, ito kakaiba po talaga.

**[02:01:09.750] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002559)  
In fact,

**[02:01:10.480] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002560)  
in the international community

**[02:01:12.920] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002561)  
of supreme audit institutions po,

**[02:01:15.220] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002562)  
ibig sabihin, ito po yung

**[02:01:17.020] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002563)  
mga ibang komisyon o opisina

**[02:01:19.000] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002564)  
sa buong mundo, they recognize

**[02:01:21.600] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002565)  
that program po.

**[02:01:23.600] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002566)  
As a program

**[02:01:24.760] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002567)  
that should be emulated po sa

**[02:01:26.440] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002568)  
ibang bansa. So malaking

**[02:01:28.620] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002569)  
bagay po sa amin yung CPA.

**[02:01:30.640] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002570)  
Pagdating naman po doon sa mga complaints,

**[02:01:32.800] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002571)  
siniseryoso po ng COA

**[02:01:34.740] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002572)  
yung mga complaints.

**[02:01:36.600] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002573)  
May mga, pagdating

**[02:01:38.880] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002574)  
sa infrastructure projects nga po,

**[02:01:41.120] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002575)  
ang ginagawa po ng mga

**[02:01:42.700] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002576)

**[02:01:45.380] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002577)  
nila yung mga poster

**[02:01:46.800] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002578)  
ng nakalagay po yung

**[02:01:48.860] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002579)

**[02:01:51.060] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002580)

**[02:01:53.600] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002581)

**[02:01:58.520] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002583)  
At pinapadala ho yun sa amin.

**[02:02:01.340] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002584)  
In our audit of the

**[02:02:04.180] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002585)  
DPWH, kasama ho sa

**[02:02:05.300] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002586)

**[02:02:10.600] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002589)  
the geo-tagging,

**[02:02:13.100] COA Commissioner Douglas Michael N. Mallillin** (asr-002590)  
outside the actual audits, we get

sa publiko.

**[02:02:19.760] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-002593)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair. Thank you, Commissioner,

**[02:02:22.400] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-002594)  
for that response.

the increasing complexity

and other emerging

**[02:02:50.620] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-002609)

**[02:02:52.580] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-002610)  
data analysts,

forensic

**[02:02:54.520] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-002612)  
auditors,

**[02:02:56.260] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-002613)  
and other technical experts?

**[02:02:57.420] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-002614)  
But precisely because COA

**[02:02:59.320] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-002615)  
is our constitutional

**[02:03:01.400] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-002616)

**[02:03:03.220] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-002617)  
I believe, Mr. Chair,

**[02:03:05.560] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-002618)

to the challenges that government

**[02:03:15.680] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-002623)  
faces today. Mr. Chair,

**[02:03:18.280] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-002624)  
it's now more than ever

**[02:03:19.840] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-002625)

**[02:03:24.180] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-002626)  
my colleagues,

But the moment the agency po
